Conclusion: RAG+Prompt翻译系统以可解释和可审计的方式改善了含NMCC的日汉句子翻译质量。局限包括单一基座模型、单一指标、依赖已发表文本和商业API；未来将拓展体裁、语言对和评估指标。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on high-resource pairs but are less reliable for Japanese-Chinese sentences containing noun-modifying clause constructions (NMCCs). This study evaluates a retrieval-augmented generation RAG+Prompt translation system that integrates linguistic analysis, embedding-based retrieval, prompt construction, and LLM generation without modifying the base model. The analysis module outputs A1 (inner vs. outer NMCC) and A2 (risk predictions: lexical choice/NMCC handling/word order/style/register); top-k = 5 similar Ja-Zh examples (L2 distance) and A1/A2 are inserted into an enhanced prompt. Using GPT-4o and a 66-sentence test set, we compare six knowledge-base sizes (0/100/200/500/1,000/2,000). Macro-averaged sentence-level BLEU (1-4-gram with brevity penalty; cased; Chinese at the character level) is the sole metric. Mean BLEU increases from 24.28 at 0 (RAG disabled) to 29.96 at 2,000 (+5.68; +23.4%). The upward trend holds across sizes, with larger knowledge bases yielding higher scores. We conclude that the RAG+Prompt translation system improves Ja-Zh translation of sentences containing NMCCs in an interpretable and auditable manner. Limitations include one base model, one metric, and reliance on published texts and commercial APIs; future work will broaden genres, language pairs, and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [75] [Geolocating News about Extreme Climate Events: A Comparative Analysis of Off-the-Shelf Tools for Toponym Identification in German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03414)
*Brielen Madureira,Mariana Madruga de Brito,Andreas Niekler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了Flair、Spacy和Stanza三种NER工具在德语新闻文章中识别极端气候事件地理位置的性能差异，并评估了这些差异对下游任务（如确定文档地理焦点和国家媒体显著性）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本中确定极端气候事件和灾害的地理位置是气候影响与适应研究中的常见问题，但不同NER工具的输出差异可能影响下游结论，亟需系统比较。

Method: 对三种现成的NER工具（Flair、Spacy、Stanza）进行对比分析，量化它们在德语新闻文章输出上的差异，并通过三种方法（判断事件发生国家）进行外在评估。

Result: 不同NER工具的输出差异会传播到下游任务中，导致关于文档地理焦点的不同决策，进而影响对各国在德语媒体中显著性的结论。

Conclusion: NER工具的选择会显著影响气候事件地理定位的准确性及后续分析结论，需谨慎对待工具差异带来的偏差。

Abstract: Determining the geolocation of extreme climate events and disasters in texts is a common problem in climate impact and adaptation research. Named-entity recognition (NER) tools are typically used to identify a pool of toponyms that serve as candidate event locations. In this study, we conduct a comparative analysis of three off-the-shelf NER tools, namely Flair, Spacy and Stanza. We describe and quantify differences between their outputs for German news articles and evaluate them extrinsically based on three methods to determine the country where events took place. We show how their contrasts are propagated into downstream tasks and can yield distinct decisions about a document's geographical focus, which, in turn, can impact conclusions about countries' prominence in German media.

</details>


### [76] [Benchmarking Logistic Regression, SVM, Naive Bayes, and IndoBERT Fine-Tuning for Sentiment Analysis on Indonesian Product Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03439)
*Nabila Zakiyah Zahra,Salwa Farhanatussaidah,Nasywa Nur Afifah,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在印尼Tokopedia产品评论数据集上，对比了传统机器学习方法（TF-IDF+逻辑回归/线性SVM/多项式朴素贝叶斯）与Transformer深度学习方法（IndoBERT）在三类情感分析任务中的表现。实验发现线性SVM以97.60%的准确率和0.5510的宏F1分数显著优于IndoBERT（88.70%/0.5088），性能差异主要源于数据采样策略不同（基线使用全量数据，Transformer使用子集）。模型最终被部署为Gradio Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印尼电商平台产生海量用户评论，传统方法难以有效处理大规模情感分析任务，且电商评论存在严重的类别不平衡问题。因此，需要系统比较传统机器学习与深度学习模型在该场景下的性能表现，并探索不平衡学习的有效策略。

Method: 采用TF-IDF特征提取结合逻辑回归、线性SVM、多项式朴素贝叶斯作为基线；微调IndoBERT模型进行上下文序列分类。针对类别不平衡，对基线模型使用平衡类权重，对IndoBERT设计自定义加权交叉熵损失函数。评估指标包括准确率、宏F1和加权F1。

Result: 线性SVM表现最优：准确率97.60%，宏F1 0.5510；IndoBERT准确率88.70%，宏F1 0.5088。性能差距主要源于数据采样差异——基线使用完整数据集，Transformer仅使用采样子集。

Conclusion: 在资源受限或数据采样不均的场景下，经过良好特征工程的传统机器学习模型（如线性SVM）在电商评论情感分析任务中可能依然优于大型预训练Transformer模型。同时验证了将模型部署为Gradio Web应用的实用可行性。

Abstract: The exponential growth of e-commerce platforms in Indonesia has generated a massive volume of user-generated product reviews. Analyzing the sentiment of these reviews is critical for measuring customer satisfaction and identifying product issues at scale. This paper benchmarks traditional Machine Learning (ML) approaches against a Transformer-based Deep Learning model for a three-class sentiment analysis task (positive, neutral, negative) on the Tokopedia Product Reviews 2025 dataset. We implemented Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency (TF-IDF) feature extraction coupled with three algorithms: Logistic Regression, Linear Support Vector Machine (SVM), and Multinomial Naive Bayes as robust baselines. Subsequently, we fine-tuned the IndoBERT model (indobenchmark/indobert-base-p1) for contextual sequence classification. To computationally address the severe class imbalance inherent in e-commerce feedback, we applied balanced class weights for the baseline models and engineered a custom weighted cross-entropy loss function within the IndoBERT training loop, following the broader motivation of imbalanced-learning research. Our comprehensive evaluation using Accuracy, Macro F1-score, and Weighted F1-score revealed that the traditional Linear SVC model significantly outperformed the IndoBERT model in our experimental setup, achieving an Accuracy of 97.60% and a Macro F1-score of 0.5510, compared to IndoBERT's 88.70% and 0.5088. Detailed analysis indicates that this performance gap was primarily driven by discrepancies in the data sampling regimes, where baselines utilized the full corpus while the Transformer was constrained to a sampled subset. Finally, we demonstrate the practical viability of our pipeline by deploying the final sentiment classification model as an interactive Gradio web application.

</details>


### [77] [A Comparison of Traditional Machine Learning Algorithms and LSTM-Based Deep Learning Models for Email Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03440)
*Virdio Samuel Saragih,Baruna Abirawa,Kartini Lovian Simbolon,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对比了SVM、逻辑回归、朴素贝叶斯与LSTM在邮件分类和情感检测中的性能。使用Word2Vec嵌入，线性核SVM以98.74%的准确率最高效，LSTM召回率优异但计算成本高。结论认为SVM在精度与速度间取得最佳平衡，适用于自动化邮件过滤系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子通信的快速增长需要更鲁棒的邮件分类和情感检测系统，现有方法在效率和准确性之间缺乏系统性对比。

Method: 采用Word2Vec进行特征表示，对比SVM（线性核）、逻辑回归、朴素贝叶斯等传统机器学习算法与LSTM深度学习架构，并通过混淆矩阵进行详细评估。

Result: 线性核SVM达到98.74%的最高准确率；LSTM在垃圾邮件相关情感检测中召回率优异，但计算时间显著高于传统统计模型；传统分类器在稠密向量空间中保持高度鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SVM在预测精度与处理速度之间提供了最优平衡，为专业和学术环境中高性能自动化邮件过滤系统的开发提供了关键见解。

Abstract: The rapid growth of electronic communication has necessitated more robust systems for email classification and sentiment detection. This study presents a comparative performance analysis between traditional machine learning algorithms and deep learning architectures, specifically focusing on Support Vector Machines (SVMs), Logistic Regression, Naive Bayes, and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM). Utilizing Word2Vec embeddings for feature representation, our experimental results indicate that the SVM model with a linear kernel achieves the highest efficiency and accuracy, reaching a peak performance of 98.74%. While the LSTM model demonstrates exceptional recall capabilities in detecting spam-related sentiments, it requires significantly more computational time compared to discriminative statistical models. Detailed evaluations via confusion matrices further reveal that traditional classifiers remain highly robust for dense vector spaces. This research concludes that for email detection tasks, SVM offers the most optimal balance between predictive precision and processing speed. These findings provide critical insights for developing high-performance automated email filtering systems in professional and academic environments.

</details>


### [78] [An ERP Study of Recursive Possessive Parsing in ASD Children and Its Cognitive Neuro Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03447)
*Fu Chenxi,Wang Xiaoyi,Zhuang Ziman,Yang Caimei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过ERP实验发现，ASD儿童在处理递归所有格结构时，早期知觉加工（P200）和句法再分析（P600）存在显著缺陷，而词汇-语义加工（N400）相对保留，支持语言模块分离假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归结构是人类语言的核心属性，但自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）儿童如何处理复杂递归的神经机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用句图匹配范式的ERP研究，比较12名ASD儿童和12名TD儿童处理两级递归所有格结构时的P200、N400和P600成分。

Result: ASD儿童P200波幅显著降低且缺乏典型后部语法效应；两组均无稳健N400违例效应，但ASD儿童表现出反转的前部效应和减弱的后部效应；ASD儿童P600波幅显著降低、无后部语法效应且潜伏期延迟。

Conclusion: ASD儿童的词汇-语义加工相对保留，但递归所需的在线句法计算严重受损，支持ASD语言能力的模块分离假说。

Abstract: Recursive structures are a core property of human language, yet little is known about how children with autism spectrum disorder (ASD) process complex recursion. This ERP study investigated the online processing of two-level recursive possessive structures in Mandarin-speaking children with ASD (n = 12) compared to typically developing (TD) peers (n = 12) using a sentence-picture matching paradigm. ERPs were analyzed for P200 (150-250 ms), N400 (300-500 ms), and P600 (500-1000 ms). Results showed that ASD children exhibited significantly reduced P200 amplitudes and failed to show the typical posterior grammaticality effect, indicating atypical early perceptual processing. No robust N400 violation effect was observed in either group, confirming the mismatch was not a semantic anomaly; however, ASD children showed a reversed anterior effect and an attenuated posterior effect. For the P600, ASD children had significantly reduced amplitudes, no posterior grammaticality effect, and a trend toward delayed latency, reflecting a core deficit in syntactic reanalysis. These findings demonstrate that while lexical-semantic processing is relatively preserved in ASD, the online syntactic computation required for recursion is severely impaired, supporting modular dissociation accounts of language in autism.

</details>


### [79] [Retrieving Floods without Floodlights: Topic Models as Binary Classifiers for Extreme Climate Events in German News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03450)
*Brielen Madureira,Mariana Madruga de Brito,Andreas Niekler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索如何将主题模型作为二分类器，用于改进德语媒体中七类极端气候事件相关新闻的检索，无需修改训练过程即可利用后验分布选择相关文档，并与微调文本嵌入分类器和开源大语言模型进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在媒体对极端气候事件的覆盖研究中，NLP方法不可或缺，但标注数据不足难以从头训练精确的深度学习分类器。主题模型虽无监督且可解释，却通常仅用于探索性分析。因此需要研究如何将主题模型用作二分类器来改进新闻检索。

Method: 利用主题模型估计的后验分布选择相关文档，不修改训练过程；通过标注样本指导评估，利用查询关键词概率选择相关主题以提高样本精确率；与微调文本嵌入分类器和开源大语言模型进行对比。

Result: 主题模型分配的关键词概率对选择相关主题具有信息性并能提高样本精确率；大语言模型精确率最低；结果具有灾害依赖性，不同类型气候事件表现不同。

Conclusion: 主题模型可有效用作二分类器改进极端气候事件新闻检索，但气候事件不应在NLP任务中被视为单一类别，需区分灾害类型。

Abstract: In studies of media coverage of extreme climate events, NLP methods have become indispensable for identifying relevant texts in large news databases. Still, enough annotated data to train accurate deep learning-based classifiers from scratch is often not available. Topic Models have the advantage of being both unsupervised and interpretable, but are typically used only for exploratory analysis or data characterisation. In this study, we investigate how to employ Topic Models as binary classifiers for refining the retrieval of relevant news about seven types of extreme climate events in the German media. Our method relies on the posterior distributions estimated by Topic Models to select relevant documents, without modifying their training procedure. Using an annotated sample to guide the evaluation, we show that the probabilities assigned to keywords used to query news databases can also be informative for selecting relevant topics and improve sample precision. We compare our results to a fine-tuned text embedding classifier and an open-weight LLM, discussing observed trade-offs, e.g. the LLM's lowest precision. Moreover, we show that results are hazard-dependent, which speaks against considering climate events as a single category in NLP tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Detecting Stealth Sycophancy in Mental-Health Dialogue with Dynamic Emotional Signature Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03472)
*Tianze Han,Beining Xu,Hanbo Zhang,Yongming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Dynamic Emotional Signature Graphs (DESG)，一种不依赖大语言模型作为最终评判者的多领域支持性对话评估方法。DESG通过解耦临床状态的对话窗口表示和不对称临床几何评分，在3000个对话窗口基准上达到0.9353 macro-F1，显著优于直接LLM评判和对称文本相似度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话评估方法（直接LLM评判和对称文本相似度指标）与治疗质量的对齐性差，因为目标标签取决于临床方向（调节、重构、恶化），而非简单的文本相似性或语义判断。

Method: 提出Dynamic Emotional Signature Graphs (DESG)，一种模型无关的评估器。核心思路是将对话窗口表示为解耦的临床状态，并使用不对称临床几何进行评分，从而捕捉治疗性对话中的临床方向性变化。

Result: 在包含3000个对话窗口的诊断性压力测试基准上，DESG-Ensemble在600窗口的保留测试集上达到0.9353 macro-F1，分别超过ConcatANN 1.51个百分点、BERTScore 19.63个百分点、TRACT 33.81个百分点。

Conclusion: 临床状态流形是主要的判别基础，图基轨迹组件提供不对称评分和可解释的诊断信息，而非性能的唯一来源。特征消融、人工控制、盲审和定性不一致案例分析均验证了这一结论。

Abstract: As conversational AI therapists are increasingly used in psychological support settings, reliable offline evaluation of therapeutic response quality remains an open problem. This paper studies multi-domain support-dialogue evaluation without relying on large language models as final judges. We use a direct LLM judge as a baseline that reads raw dialogue text and predicts whether the target response is harmful, productive, or neutral. We find that direct LLM judges and symmetric text-similarity metrics are poorly aligned with therapeutic quality because the target label depends on clinical direction: whether the response moves the user state toward regulation or reframing, leaves it broadly unchanged, or reinforces deterioration through higher risk affect or cognitive-distortion mass. To address this issue, we propose Dynamic Emotional Signature Graphs (DESG), a model-agnostic evaluator that represents dialogue windows with decoupled clinical states and scores them using asymmetric clinical geometry. We evaluate DESG on a constructed diagnostic stress-test benchmark of 3{,}000 dialogue windows from EmpatheticDialogues, ESConv, and CRADLE-Dialogue, covering peer support, counseling dialogue, and crisis-oriented interaction. On the 600-window held-out test aggregate, DESG-Ensemble achieves 0.9353 macro-F1, exceeding ConcatANN by 1.51 percentage points, BERTScore by 19.63 points, and TRACT by 33.81 points. Feature ablations, artifact controls, a 100-window blinded adjudicator audit, and qualitative disagreement cases indicate that the clinical state manifold is the main discriminative substrate, while graph-based trajectory components provide asymmetric scoring and interpretable diagnostics rather than serving as the sole source of performance.

</details>


### [81] [CuraView: A Multi-Agent Framework for Medical Hallucination Detection with GraphRAG-Enhanced Knowledge Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03476)
*Severin Ye,Xiao Kong,Xiaopeng He,Guangsu Yan,Dongsuk Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CuraView是一个多智能体框架，通过构建基于GraphRAG的患者级知识图谱和闭环生成-检测流水线，实现对出院小结中忠实性幻觉的句子级检测与证据链解释。在Discharge-Me基准测试中，微调后的Qwen3-14B模型在安全关键指标E4上达到0.831的F1值，较基线模型提升50.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出院小结需要从冗长的电子健康记录（EHR）中提取关键信息，手动提取耗时费力；大语言模型虽能提升生成效率，但容易产生忠实性幻觉（与源记录矛盾的陈述），直接威胁患者安全。

Method: 提出CuraView多智能体框架，包含：(1) 基于GraphRAG构建患者级知识图谱；(2) 闭环生成-检测流水线，进行句子级证据检索与分类；(3) 四种证据等级（E1强支持至E4直接矛盾）的结构化可解释证据链。

Result: 在Discharge-Me基准的250名患者子集（50名测试）上，微调Qwen3-14B模型在E4指标上F1=0.831（召回率90.9%，精确率76.5%），E3+E4上F1=0.823，较基线模型相对提升50.0%，优于RAGTruth-style和QAGS-style基线。

Conclusion: 基于证据链的图检索验证能显著提升临床文档的事实可靠性，同时可生成可复用的标注数据集用于下游模型训练与蒸馏。

Abstract: Discharge summaries require extracting critical information from lengthy electronic health records (EHRs), a process that is labor-intensive when performed manually. Large language models (LLMs) can improve generation efficiency; however, they are prone to producing faithfulness hallucinations, statements that contradict source records, posing direct risks to patient safety. To address this, we present CuraView, a multi-agent framework for sentence-level detection and evidence-grounded explanation of faithfulness hallucinations in discharge summaries. CuraView constructs a GraphRAG-based knowledge graph from patient-level EHRs and implements a closed-loop generation-detection pipeline with sentence-level evidence retrieval and classification spanning four evidence grades from strong support to direct contradiction (E1-E4), yielding structured and interpretable evidence chains.
  We evaluate CuraView on a subset of 250 patients from the Discharge-Me benchmark, with 50 patients held out for testing. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-14B detection model achieves an F1 of 0.831 on the safety-critical E4 metric (90.9% recall, 76.5% precision) and an F1 of 0.823 on E3+E4, representing a 50.0% relative improvement over the base model and outperforming RAGTruth-style and QAGS-style baselines. These results demonstrate that evidence-chain-based graph retrieval verification substantially improves the factual reliability of clinical documentation, while simultaneously producing reusable annotated datasets for downstream model training and distillation.

</details>


### [82] [Revisiting Graph-Tokenizing Large Language Models: A Systematic Evaluation of Graph Token Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03514)
*Zhongjian Zhang,Yue Yu,Mengmei Zhang,Junping Du,Xiao Wang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了图分词LLM（GTokenLLM）能够有效理解图嵌入的普遍观点，提出统一框架GTEval评估图分词理解能力。实验发现现有GTokenLLM对指令变化过度敏感或不敏感、严重依赖文本推理、且图分词在不同层利用率不一，额外指令微调虽能提升性能但无法根本解决理解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑现有GTokenLLM是否真正理解图分词在自然语言嵌入空间中的语义，而非仅仅依赖文本上下文进行推理。

Method: 提出统一框架GTEval，通过格式级和内容级指令变换评估图分词理解能力，在6个代表性模型上进行实验。

Result: (1)现有GTokenLLM未完全理解图分词，表现出过度敏感/不敏感，依赖文本推理；(2)图分词保留图信息并在各层被关注，但利用率因模型和指令而异；(3)指令微调提升性能但未根本解决图分词理解问题。

Conclusion: 现有GTokenLLM未能真正理解图分词，需要进一步改进以增强图-语言对齐理解能力。

Abstract: The remarkable success of large language models (LLMs) has motivated researchers to adapt them as universal predictors for various graph tasks. As a widely recognized paradigm, Graph-Tokenizing LLMs (GTokenLLMs) compress complex graph data into graph tokens and treat them as prefix tokens for querying LLMs, leading many to believe that LLMs can understand graphs more effectively and efficiently. In this paper, we challenge this belief: \textit{Do GTokenLLMs fully understand graph tokens in the natural-language embedding space?} Motivated by this question, we formalize a unified framework for GTokenLLMs and propose an evaluation pipeline, \textbf{GTEval}, to assess graph-token understanding via instruction transformations at the format and content levels. We conduct extensive experiments on 6 representative GTokenLLMs with GTEval. The primary findings are as follows: (1) Existing GTokenLLMs do not fully understand graph tokens. They exhibit over-sensitivity or over-insensitivity to instruction changes, and rely heavily on text for reasoning; (2) Although graph tokens preserve task-relevant graph information and receive attention across LLM layers, their utilization varies across models and instruction variants; (3) Additional instruction tuning can improve performance on the original and seen instructions, but it does not fully address the challenge of graph-token understanding, calling for further improvement.

</details>


### [83] [SURE-RAG: Sufficiency and Uncertainty-Aware Evidence Verification for Selective Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03534)
*Jingxi Qiu,Zeyu Han,Cheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SURE-RAG提出了一种透明的聚合协议，通过成对声明-证据验证器生成局部关系分布，进而聚合为可解释的答案级信号（覆盖率、关系强度、分歧、冲突、检索不确定性），实现证据充分性验证的三元决策（支持/反驳/不足），在HotpotQA-RAG v3基准上达到0.9075 Macro-F1，显著优于基线方法，并提供了完全的可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统中，检索到的段落虽然相关但未必能充分支持答案，即存在"检索≠验证"的差距。证据充分性是集合级属性，独立段落评分无法检测缺失的推理跳步和未解决的信息冲突，因此需要一种透明的、可解释的证据充分性验证机制。

Method: SURE-RAG采用基于成对声明-证据验证器的聚合协议：首先对每个声明与每条证据进行成对验证，生成局部关系分布；然后通过聚合机制将这些局部信息转化为答案级的可解释信号（覆盖率、关系强度、分歧、冲突、检索不确定性），最终做出三元决策（支持/反驳/不足）并输出可审计的选择性分数。

Result: 在HotpotQA-RAG v3基准上，校准后的SURE-RAG达到0.9075 Macro-F1（0.8951±0.0069），远超DeBERTa均值池化（0.6516）和GPT-4o评判（0.7284），与强但黑箱的拼接交叉编码器（0.8888±0.0109）性能相当且完全可审计。30%覆盖率下的风险从0.2588降至0.1642（不安全答案减少37%）。在HaluBench不安全检测上，SURE-RAG与GPT-4o排名反转（0.3343 vs 0.7389 unsafe-F1），验证了充分性验证与自然幻觉检测是不同问题。

Conclusion: 证据充分性验证是一个与常规幻觉检测截然不同的任务，需要显式建模证据的集合级属性；SURE-RAG通过透明可解释的聚合机制，在不牺牲性能的前提下提供了全审计能力，有效降低了RAG系统在证据不充分时生成不安全答案的风险。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds answers in retrieved passages, but retrieval is not verification: a passage can be topical and still fail to justify the answer. We frame this gap as evidence sufficiency verification for selective RAG answering: given a question, a candidate answer, and retrieved evidence, predict whether the evidence supports, refutes, or is insufficient, and abstain unless support is established. We present SURE-RAG, a transparent aggregation protocol built on the observation that evidence sufficiency is a set-level property: missing hops and unresolved conflicts cannot be detected by independent passage scoring. A shared pair-level claim-evidence verifier produces local relation distributions, which SURE-RAG aggregates into interpretable answer-level signals -- coverage, relation strength, disagreement, conflict, and retrieval uncertainty -- yielding a three-way decision and an auditable selective score. We evaluate on HotpotQA-RAG v3, a controlled multi-hop benchmark, under an artifact-aware protocol (shortcut baselines, counterfactual swaps, no-oracle checks, GPT-4o audits). Calibrated SURE-RAG reaches 0.9075 Macro-F1 (0.8951 +/- 0.0069), substantially above DeBERTa mean-pooling (0.6516) and a GPT-4o judge (0.7284), while matching a strong but opaque concat cross-encoder (0.8888 +/- 0.0109) with full auditability. Risk at 30% coverage drops from 0.2588 to 0.1642, a 37% reduction in unsafe answers. To deliberately probe the task boundary, we further contrast SURE-RAG with GPT-4o on HaluBench unsafe detection: the ranking reverses (0.3343 vs 0.7389 unsafe-F1), establishing that controlled sufficiency verification and natural hallucination detection are distinct problems.

</details>


### [84] [PatRe: A Full-Stage Office Action and Rebuttal Generation Benchmark for Patent Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03571)
*Qiyao Wang,Xinyi Chen,Longze Chen,Hongbo Wang,Hamid Alinejad-Rokny,Yuan Lin,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出首个将专利审查建模为动态多轮交互过程（审查意见生成与申请人答辩）的基准PatRe，包含480个真实案例，支持oracle与检索模拟两种评估设置，揭示了LLM在此任务上的能力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将专利审查视为判别式分类或静态抽取任务，未能捕捉其内在的交互式与迭代式特性（类似学术同行评审与反驳过程）。

Method: 构建PatRe基准，包含480个真实专利审查案例，将审查建模为多轮论证与回应过程（审查意见通知书生成与申请人答辩），支持oracle与检索模拟两种评估设置。

Result: 实验揭示闭源与开源模型间的性能差异，以及审查员分析与申请人答辩之间的任务不对称性。

Conclusion: LLMs在建模复杂的现实世界法律推理与专利审查技术新颖性判断方面既有潜力也存在当前局限。

Abstract: Patent examination is a complex, multi-stage process requiring both technical expertise and legal reasoning, increasingly challenged by rising application volumes. Prior benchmarks predominantly view patent examination as discriminative classification or static extraction, failing to capture its inherently interactive and iterative nature, similar to the peer review and rebuttal process in academic publishing. In this paper, we introduce PatRe, the first benchmark that models the full patent examination lifecycle, including Office Action generation and applicant rebuttal. PatRe comprises 480 real-world cases and supports both oracle and retrieval-simulated evaluation settings. Our benchmark reframes patent examination as a dynamic, multi-turn process of justification and response. Extensive experiments across various LLMs reveal critical insights into model performance, including differences between proprietary and open-source models, as well as task asymmetries between examiner analysis and applicant-side rebuttal. These findings highlight both the potential and current limitations of LLMs in modeling complex, real-world legal reasoning and technical novelty judgment in patent examination. We release our code and dataset to facilitate future research on patent examination modeling.

</details>


### [85] [BIT.UA-AAUBS at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Evaluating Open-Source and Proprietary LLMs via Prompting in Low-Resource QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03618)
*Richard A. A. Jonker,Alexander Christiansen,Alexandros Maniatis,Rúben Garrido,Rogério Braunschweiger de Freitas Lima,Roman Jurowetzki,Sérgio Matos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BIT.UA和AAUBS团队在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中的参与情况，针对低资源环境下的临床问答和证据溯源问题，在不更新权重的条件下利用多种提示工程策略评估了多个专有模型和开源模型，最终在子任务4（证据引用对齐）中获得第一名，在子任务3（患者友好答案生成）中获得第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源医疗场景下，由于训练数据缺失和GDPR等严格数据隐私约束，无法进行模型微调，因此需要探索无需权重更新的LLM提示工程方法来实现临床问答与证据溯源。

Method: 采用多种提示工程策略（包括任务分解、思维链、上下文学习）评估多个最先进的专有模型和本地可部署的开源模型，并探索多数投票和LLM-as-a-judge集成技术以提升预测鲁棒性。

Result: 专有模型对提示变化表现出强鲁棒性；领域适配的开源模型（如MedGemma 3 27B）在正确提示下取得极具竞争力的性能。最终在子任务4（证据引用对齐）中获得第一名，在子任务3（患者友好答案生成）中获得第三名。

Conclusion: 基于提示的方法在低资源临床问答任务中高度有效，无需权重更新即可取得领先性能，代码和提示均已开源。

Abstract: This paper presents the joint participation of the BIT.UA and AAUBS groups in the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task, which focuses on clinical question answering and evidence grounding in a low-resource setting. Due to the absence of training data and the strict data privacy constraints inherent to the healthcare domain (e.g. GDPR), we investigate the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) without weight updates. We evaluate several state-of-the-art proprietary models and locally deployable open-source alternatives using various prompt engineering strategies, including task decomposition, Chain-of-Thought, and in-context learning. Furthermore, we explore majority voting and LLM-as-a-judge ensembling techniques to maximize predictive robustness. Our results demonstrate that while proprietary models exhibit strong resilience to prompt variations, domain-adapted open-source models (such as MedGemma 3 27B) achieve highly competitive performance when paired with the right prompt. Overall, our prompt-based approach proved highly effective, securing 1st place in Subtask 4 (evidence citation alignment) and 3rd place in Subtask 3 (patient-friendly answer generation). All code, results, and prompts are available on our GitHub repository: https://github.com/bioinformatics-ua/ArchEHR-QA-2026.

</details>


### [86] [Annotation Quality in Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis: A Case Study Comparing Experts, Students, Crowdworkers, and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03624)
*Niklas Donhauser,Jakob Fehle,Nils Constantin Hellwig,Markus Weinberger,Udo Kruschwitz,Christian Wolff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同标注来源（专家、学生、众包工人、大语言模型）对德文方面级情感分析的影响，通过对比标注一致性和下游模型性能，为低资源场景下的数据集构建提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德文ABS研究因缺乏高质量标注数据集而受限，现有研究多集中于英文，亟需探索不同标注来源对德文ABS数据集质量的影响。

Method: 将现有数据集由专家重新标注作为ground truth，对比学生、众包工人、LLM和专家的标注质量；使用IAA衡量一致性，评估对ACSA和TASD两个子任务的下游模型性能影响；采用BERT、T5、LLaMA等SOTA方法进行微调和上下文学习。

Result: 不同标注来源在IAA和下游任务性能上存在显著差异，专家标注质量最高但成本高，LLM在效率与可靠性间取得较好平衡。

Conclusion: 研究为低资源NLP场景下的数据集构建提供了标注来源选择的实用指导，揭示了标注可靠性与效率之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) enables fine-grained opinion analysis by identifying sentiments toward specific aspects or targets within a text. While ABSA has been widely studied for English, research on other languages such as German remains limited, largely due to the lack of high-quality annotated datasets. This paper examines how different annotation sources influence the development of German ABSA. To this end, an existing dataset is re-annotated by experts to establish a ground truth, which serves as a reference for evaluating annotations produced by students, crowdworkers, Large Language Models (LLMs), and experts. Annotation quality is compared using Inter-Annotator Agreement (IAA) and its impact on downstream model performance for different ABSA subtasks. The evaluation focuses on Aspect Category Sentiment Analysis (ACSA) and Target Aspect Sentiment Detection (TASD). We apply State-of-the-Art (SOTA) methods for ABSA, including BERT-, T5-, and LLaMA-based approaches to assess performance differences, spanning fine-tuning and in-context learning with instruction prompts. The findings provide practical insights into trade-offs between annotation reliability and efficiency, offering guidance for dataset construction in under-resourced Natural Language Processing (NLP) scenarios.

</details>


### [87] [A Paradigm for Interpreting Metrics and Identifying Critical Errors in Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03671)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出最小编辑距离（minED）范式，将人类感知度量融入传统编辑距离计算，从而获得既反映人类感知又可解释的误识率等价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WER和CER等传统评估指标与人类感知相关性差，且无法捕捉语言和语义信息；而基于度量的嵌入方法虽能逼近人类感知，但其分数难以像WER/CER那样直观解释。

Method: 提出一种新范式，将选定的度量（metric）融入最小编辑距离（minED）中，从而获得一个等价于误识率的可解释分数。

Result: minED能够将转录错误与人类感知对齐，并支持从人类角度对错误严重性进行原始分析。

Conclusion: 该范式克服了传统指标不可感知和新指标不可解释的双重缺陷，为语音转录评估提供了一种兼顾可解释性与人类感知对齐的有效方案。

Abstract: The most commonly used metrics for evaluating automatic speech transcriptions, namely Word Error Rate (WER) and Character Error Rate (CER), have been heavily criticized for their poor correlation to human perception and their inability to take into account linguistic and semantic information. While metric-based embeddings, seeking to approximate human perception, have been proposed, their scores remain difficult to interpret, unlike WER and CER. In this article, we overcome this problem by proposing a paradigm that consists in incorporating a chosen metric into it in order to obtain an equivalent of the error rate: a Minimum Edit Distance (minED). This approach parallels transcription errors with their human perception, also allowing an original study of the severity of these errors from a human perspective.

</details>


### [88] [A Comprehensive Analysis of Tokenization and Self-Supervised Learning in End-to-End Automatic Speech Recognition applied on French Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03696)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对法语端到端自动语音识别系统，研究了子词分词算法和自监督学习模型在不同语言和声学维度上的影响，使用了多种评估指标，超越了传统的CER和WER指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR评估仅依赖CER和WER指标，但这些指标不足以全面描述自动转录在下游应用中的表现，存在评估不完整的问题。

Method: 对法语进行定性研究，从不同语言和声学角度出发，使用全面的评估指标集，分析子词分词算法和自监督学习模型的影响。

Result: 研究发现超参数和模型选择对ASR性能至关重要，且传统CER/WER指标无法充分反映下游应用表现。

Conclusion: 需要采用更全面的评估指标来指导ASR系统的超参数和模型选择，以更好地适应实际应用需求。

Abstract: The performance of end-to-end automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems enables their increasing integration into numerous applications. While there are various benefits to such speech-to-text systems, the choice of hyperparameters and models plays a crucial role in their performance. Typically, these choices are determined by considering only the character (CER) and/or word error rate (WER) metrics. However, it has been shown in several studies that these metrics are largely incomplete and fail to adequately describe the downstream application of automatic transcripts. In this paper, we conduct a qualitative study on the French language that investigates the impact of subword tokenization algorithms and self-supervised learning models from different linguistic and acoustic perspectives, using a comprehensive set of evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [89] [SERE: Structural Example Retrieval for Enhancing LLMs in Event Causality Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03701)
*Zhifeng Hao,Zhongjie Chen,Junhao Lu,Shengyin Yu,Guimin Hu,Keli Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Boyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SERE框架，通过基于概念路径度量、句法度量和因果模式过滤的结构化示例检索，增强大语言模型在事件因果识别任务中的表现，有效缓解因果幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在事件因果识别中因果推理存在偏差，容易过度预测因果关系（即因果幻觉），导致ECI任务性能受限。

Method: 提出SERE框架，利用三种结构化检索策略：(i) 概念路径度量——基于ConceptNet编辑距离衡量事件间概念关系；(ii) 句法度量——通过句法树编辑距离量化结构相似性；(iii) 因果模式过滤——利用LLM基于预定义因果结构过滤示例。通过集成这些策略选择更相关的示例来引导LLM进行因果推理。

Result: 在多个ECI数据集上的大量实验验证了SERE的有效性，能够缓解因果推理偏差并提高ECI任务准确率。

Conclusion: SERE通过结构化示例检索有效缓解了LLM在ECI任务中的因果幻觉问题，提升了因果推理的准确性。

Abstract: Event Causality Identification (ECI) requires models to determine whether a given pair of events in a context exhibits a causal relationship. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across various NLP tasks, their effectiveness in ECI remains limited due to biases in causal reasoning, often leading to overprediction of causal relationships (causal hallucination). To mitigate these issues and enhance LLM performance in ECI, we propose SERE, a structural example retrieval framework that leverages LLMs' few-shot learning capabilities. SERE introduces an innovative retrieval mechanism based on three structural concepts: (i) Conceptual Path Metric, which measures the conceptual relationship between events using edit distance in ConceptNet; (ii) Syntactic Metric, which quantifies structural similarity through tree edit distance on syntactic trees; and (iii) Causal Pattern Filtering, which filters examples based on predefined causal structures using LLMs. By integrating these structural retrieval strategies, SERE selects more relevant examples to guide LLMs in causal reasoning, mitigating bias and improving accuracy in ECI tasks. Extensive experiments on multiple ECI datasets validate the effectiveness of SERE. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/DMIRLAB-Group/SERE.

</details>


### [90] [SAM-NER: Semantic Archetype Mediation for Zero-Shot Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03706)
*Ruichu Cai,Juntao Gan,Miao Mai,Zhifeng Hao,Boyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAM-NER提出了一种基于语义原型中介的三阶段零样本命名实体识别框架，通过构建域不变的原型空间来稳定跨域实体识别，在CrossNER基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本NER在领域和标签体系迁移时表现脆弱，大语言模型的内在语义组织与未见过的标签定义之间存在错位，直接映射实体到细粒度标签会导致系统性语义漂移，尤其当目标标签体系新颖或语义重叠时。

Method: 三阶段框架：(i) 实体发现：通过协作提取和基于共识的去噪获得高覆盖率、高保真的实体片段；(ii) 抽象中介：将实体投影到一组从高层本体抽象中蒸馏出的通用语义原型组成的紧凑集合中；(iii) 语义校准：通过受约束的、定义对齐的推理，利用冻结的LLM将原型级预测解析为目标域类型。

Result: 在CrossNER基准上，SAM-NER在跨域设置下持续优于强基线零样本NER方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入域不变的语义原型空间作为中介，可以有效缓解零样本NER中的语义漂移问题，提升跨域迁移的稳定性。

Abstract: Zero-shot Named Entity Recognition (ZS-NER) remains brittle under domain and schema shifts, where unseen label definitions often misalign with a large language model's (LLM's) intrinsic semantic organization. As a result, directly mapping entity mentions to fine-grained target labels can induce systematic semantic drift, especially when target schemas are novel or semantically overlapping. We propose \textbf{SAM-NER}, a three-stage framework based on \emph{Semantic Archetype Mediation} that stabilizes cross-domain transfer through an intermediate, domain-invariant archetype space. SAM-NER: (i) performs \emph{Entity Discovery} via cooperative extraction and consensus-based denoising to obtain high-coverage, high-fidelity entity spans; (ii) conducts \emph{Abstract Mediation} by projecting entities into a compact set of universal semantic archetypes distilled from high-level ontological abstractions; and (iii) applies \emph{Semantic Calibration} to resolve archetype-level predictions into target-domain types through constrained, definition-aligned inference with a frozen LLM. Experiments on the CrossNER benchmark show that SAM-NER consistently outperforms strong prior ZS-NER baselines in cross-domain settings. Our implementation will be open-sourced at https://github.com/DMIRLAB-Group/SAM-NER.

</details>


### [91] [Rose-SQL: Role-State Evolution Guided Structured Reasoning for Multi-Turn Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03720)
*Le Zhou,Feng Yao,Fengcai Qiao,Bo Xu,Fangyuan Wang,Boyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Rose-SQL是一个无需训练的框架，通过上下文学习利用小型大推理模型，并引入Role-State结构表示来桥接模式链接与SQL生成，在SParC和CoSQL基准测试中超越了现有微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型在代码生成和数学推理上表现出色，但在多轮Text-to-SQL任务中的潜力尚未被充分探索；现有方法依赖不稳定的API推理或需要昂贵的小规模模型微调。

Method: 提出Rose-SQL框架，采用无需训练的上下文学习方法；引入Role-State细粒度表示作为结构蓝图，通过结构同构性检查追踪历史上下文中Role-State的演化，引导模型基于已验证的交互轨迹推断当前问题的SQL组成。

Result: 在SParC和CoSQL基准上，Qwen3系列中4B规模Rose-SQL超越上下文学习基线，8B和14B规模显著超越最先进的微调模型，并在其他推理骨干上表现一致提升。

Conclusion: Rose-SQL证明了无需额外训练即可有效利用小型LRM完成多轮Text-to-SQL任务，Role-State表示有效弥合了模式链接与SQL生成之间的结构差距。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) trained with Long Chain-of-Thought have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in code generation and mathematical reasoning. However, their potential in multi-turn Text-to-SQL tasks remains largely underexplored. Existing approaches typically rely on unstable API-based inference or require expensive fine-tuning on small-scale models. In this work, we present Rose-SQL, a training-free framework that leverages small-scale LRMs through in-context learning to enable accurate context-dependent parsing. We introduce the Role-State, a fine-grained representation that bridges the structural gap between schema linking and SQL generation by serving as a structural blueprint. To handle conversational dependencies, Rose-SQL traces the evolution of Role-State through historical context via structural isomorphism checks, guiding the model to infer the possible SQL composition for the current question through verified interaction trajectories. Experiments on the SParC and CoSQL benchmarks show that, within the Qwen3 series, Rose-SQL outperforms in-context learning baselines at the 4B scale and substantially surpasses state-of-the-art fine-tuned models at the 8B and 14B scales, while showing consistent gains on additional reasoning backbones.

</details>


### [92] [Segmenting Human-LLM Co-authored Text via Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03723)
*Mengchu Li,Jin Zhu,Jinglai Li,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将人类与LLM合著文本分割任务类比为时间序列中的变化点检测问题，通过改进的加权和广义变化点检测算法，实现了对文本中人类撰写和LLM生成片段的精确定位，并在理论上证明了方法的 minimax 最优性，实验表明优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器通常只对整段文本进行二分类，无法处理人类与LLM合著文本中需要定位具体片段的场景，亟需一种能够细粒度分割文本中人类撰写和LLM生成内容的方法。

Method: 将文本分割任务类比为时间序列中的变化点检测问题，据此设计加权算法和广义算法以适配检测分数的异方差性，并理论证明了该过程的 minimax 最优性。

Result: 在多个实验场景下，该方法在分割人类与LLM合著文本的任务中显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 变化点检测框架可有效解决人类-LLM合著文本的细粒度分割问题，所提算法兼具理论最优性与实际有效性。

Abstract: The rise of large language models (LLMs) has created an urgent need to distinguish between human-written and LLM-generated text to ensure authenticity and societal trust. Existing detectors typically provide a binary classification for an entire passage; however, this is insufficient for human--LLM co-authored text, where the objective is to localize specific segments authored by humans or LLMs. To bridge this gap, we propose algorithms to segment text into human- and LLM-authored pieces. Our key observation is that such a segmentation task is conceptually similar to classical change point detection in time-series analysis. Leveraging this analogy, we adapt change point detection to LLM-generated text detection, develop a weighted algorithm and a generalized algorithm to accommodate heterogeneous detection score variability, and establish the minimax optimality of our procedure. Empirically, we demonstrate the strong performance of our approach against a wide range of existing baselines.

</details>


### [93] [Natural Language Processing: A Comprehensive Practical Guide from Tokenisation to RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03799)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个系统性的、面向研究的NLP实践教程，涵盖从分词到LLM微调、RAG和RLHF的完整流程，包含12个动手实验环节，并在低资源语言（塔吉克语和鞑靼语）上进行了原创研究，所有实验均需在公共仓库中发布代码、模型和报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一种可复现的、研究导向的NLP实践教学资源，弥补传统教科书缺乏动手实践和可复现性的不足，同时推动低资源语言的NLP研究。

Method: 设计12个循序渐进的实验环节，每个环节包含精简理论、详细实现方案、形式化评估指标和透明评估标准；使用单一演进语料库；采用开源模型而非商业API；依托Hugging Face生态系统；在塔吉克语和鞑靼语上构建子词分词器、嵌入、词汇数据库和音译基准。

Result: 构建了一套完整的、可复现的NLP实践教程，并在低资源语言上产出了具体的语言资源（分词器、嵌入、词汇数据库、音译基准）。

Conclusion: 该工作可作为高级本科生、研究生和从业者从经典ML到前沿LLM系统的实践指南，尤其展示了如何在数据稀缺环境下适配现代NLP技术。

Abstract: This preprint presents a systematic, research-oriented practicum that guides the reader through the entire modern NLP pipeline: from tokenisation and vectorisation to fine-tuning of large language models, retrieval-augmented generation, and reinforcement learning from human feedback. Twelve hands-on sessions combine concise theory with detailed implementation plans, formalised evaluation metrics, and transparent assessment criteria. The work is not a conventional textbook: it is designed as a reproducible research artefact where every session requires publishing code, models, and reports in public repositories. All experiments are conducted on a single evolving corpus, and the work advocates open-weight models over commercial APIs, with special attention to the Hugging Face ecosystem. The material is enriched by original research on low-resource languages, incorporating linguistic resources for Tajik and Tatar (subword tokenisers, embeddings, lexical databases, and transliteration benchmarks), demonstrating how modern NLP can be adapted to data-scarce environments. Designed for senior undergraduates, graduate students, and practising developers seeking to implement, compare, and deploy methods from classical ML to state-of-the-art LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [94] [Reproducing Complex Set-Compositional Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03824)
*Vincent Degenhart,Dewi Timman,Arjen P. de Vries,Faegheh Hasibi,Mohanna Hoveyda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过重现性研究，发现现有神经检索器在处理集合组合查询时存在严重缺陷：在标准基准QUEST上表现优异（Recall@100>0.41），但在新提出的LIMIT+基准上性能骤降（低于0.02），而经典词法检索方法却能达到~0.96。随着组合深度增加，密集方法性能崩溃，而稀疏和词法方法表现更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂信息需求常涉及合取、析取和排除等集合组合查询，但现有检索范式是否真正满足这些约束，还是仅仅利用了"语义捷径"，这一问题尚不明确。

Method: 对主要检索家族和针对推理的方法在QUEST和QUEST+Variants上进行重现性研究；引入LIMIT+基准，其相关性取决于任意属性谓词和约束满足，而非预训练知识；按组合深度对性能进行分层分析。

Result: (i) QUEST上神经检索器效果是BM25的两倍以上(Recall@100>0.41 vs 0.20)，但ReasonIR和Search-R1并不优于通用检索器；(ii) LIMIT+上神经方法从~0.42骤降至0.02以下，词法检索提升至~0.96；(iii) 随组合深度增加，密集方法崩溃，稀疏和词法方法更稳定。

Conclusion: 现有神经检索方法在真正需要集合组合约束满足的场景下表现极差，未能泛化其表观优势；经典词法检索和代数稀疏方法在组合查询中更可靠。需要更严格的基准来评估检索模型的实际推理能力。

Abstract: Complex information needs may involve set-compositional queries using conjunction, disjunction, and exclusion, yet it remains unclear whether current retrieval paradigms genuinely satisfy such constraints or exploit `semantic shortcuts'. We conduct a reproducibility study to benchmark major retrieval families and reasoning-targeted methods on QUEST and QUEST+Variants, and introduce LIMIT+, a controlled benchmark where relevance depends on arbitrary attribute predicates and constraint satisfaction, and less on pretrained knowledge. Our findings show that (i) on QUEST, the best neural retrievers achieve an effectiveness that is more than double what can be achieved with BM25 (Recall@100 ${>}$0.41 vs.\ 0.20), but reasoning-targeted methods like ReasonIR and Search-R1 do not outperform general-purpose retrievers uniformly; (ii) on LIMIT+, gains fail to transfer, where the strongest QUEST method collapses from Recall@100${\approx}$0.42 to below 0.02, while classic lexical retrieval gains to ${\sim}$0.96. Lastly, (iii) stratifying by compositional depth reveals a consistent degradation across all methods, where algebraic sparse and lexical methods show more stable performance while dense approaches collapse. We release code and LIMIT+ data generation scripts to support future reproducibility and controlled evaluation.

</details>


### [95] [TRACE: A Metrologically-Grounded Engineering Framework for Trustworthy Agentic AI Systems in Operationally Critical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03838)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE是一个面向关键操作领域的可信自主AI跨领域工程框架，通过四层参考架构（含显式的经典ML与LLM验证器分离L2a/L2b、有状态编排与升级策略L3、有限人类监督L4）、基于计量学的信任度量套件（对标GUM/VIM/ISO 17025）以及计算简约比（CPR）量化的模型简约原则，在临床决策、工业多域操作和司法AI助手三个截然不同的治理场景中实现了统一架构与度量的迁移，使LLM的使用成为审慎的设计决策而非架构默认值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在操作关键领域（如医疗、工业、司法）中部署可信的自主AI系统面临跨领域治理差异的挑战，现有框架缺乏统一的架构设计和可迁移的信任度量标准，尤其是对LLM的使用缺乏审慎的工程决策机制。

Method: 提出四层参考架构（L1-L4），核心创新是L2a（经典ML）与L2b（LLM验证器）的显式分离，以及有状态编排与升级策略（L3）和有限人类监督（L4）；设计基于计量学（GUM/VIM/ISO 17025）的信任度量套件；提出计算简约比（CPR）作为模型简约原则的量化指标；在三个不同治理领域进行实例化验证。

Result: 三个跨领域实例（临床决策支持、工业多域操作、司法AI助手）成功迁移了相同的架构和度量标准，证明了框架的通用性；CPR作为一阶设计原则被引入可信AI工程中；L2a/L2b分离使得LLM使用成为审慎的设计选择而非默认选项。

Conclusion: TRACE通过架构分离、计量学信任度量与CPR简约原则，为操作关键领域的可信自主AI提供了跨领域可迁移的工程化解决方案，标志着CPR作为可信AI工程中一阶设计原则的确立。

Abstract: We introduce TRACE, a cross-domain engineering framework for trustworthy agentic AI in operationally critical domains. TRACE combines a four-layer reference architecture with an explicit classical-ML vs. LLM-validator split (L2a/L2b), a stateful orchestration-and-escalation policy (L3), and bounded human supervision (L4); a metrologically grounded trust-metric suite mapped to GUM/VIM/ISO 17025; and a Model-Parsimony principle quantified by the Computational Parsimony Ratio (CPR). Three instantiations--clinical decision support, industrial multi-domain operations, and a judicial AI assistant--transfer the samearchitecture and metrics across principally different governance contexts. The L2a/L2b separation makes the use of large language models a deliberate design decision rather than an architectural default, with parsimony quantified through CPR. TRACE introduces CPR as a first-class design principle in trustworthy-AI engineering.

</details>


### [96] [MCJudgeBench: A Benchmark for Constraint-Level Judge Evaluation in Multi-Constraint Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03858)
*Jaeyun Lee,Junyoung Koh,Zeynel Tok,Hunar Batra,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MCJudgeBench，一个用于多约束指令跟随场景下约束级评估LLM裁判的基准，揭示了裁判在约束级判断中存在正确性与不一致性之间的复杂关系，且强整体性能并不保证可靠的细粒度判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM裁判评估仅关注整体响应级别的判断，忽视了在多约束指令跟随任务中约束级别判断的可靠性，缺乏细粒度、可控的评估方法。

Method: 构建MCJudgeBench基准，包含指令、候选响应、显式约束列表、每个约束的金标签（yes/partial/no）以及受控响应侧扰动。评估协议包括多种提示变体以测试稳定性，使用正确性和不一致性（内在不一致性与程序不一致性）两个指标。

Result: 强整体性能不保证约束级判断的均衡可靠性（尤其是rare的partial和no类别）；更高正确性的裁判不一定具有更低的不一致性；推理增强评估提升正确性但未稳定提升稳定性。

Conclusion: LLM裁判的可靠性是多维的，需要在约束级别进行评估以研究其失败模式，仅依赖整体响应判断不足以全面衡量裁判质量。

Abstract: Multi-constraint instruction following requires verifying whether a response satisfies multiple individual requirements, yet LLM judges are often assessed only through overall-response judgments. We introduce MCJudgeBench, a benchmark for constraint-level judge evaluation in multi-constraint instruction following. Each instance includes an instruction, a candidate response, an explicit constraint list, per-constraint gold labels in {yes, partial, no}, and controlled response-side perturbations. The evaluation protocol further includes evaluation prompt variants to test judge stability. We evaluate proprietary and open-source LLM judges using both correctness and inconsistency metrics, distinguishing intrinsic inconsistency under stochastic decoding from procedural inconsistency under prompt and response perturbations. Our results show that judge reliability has multiple dimensions: strong overall performance does not guarantee equally reliable detection across label categories, especially for rarer partial and no cases. Judges with higher correctness do not always have lower inconsistency. Evaluation with reasoning improves correctness but does not uniformly improve stability. These findings motivate evaluating LLM judges at the constraint level to study these failure modes.

</details>


### [97] [CC-OCR V2: Benchmarking Large Multimodal Models for Literacy in Real-world Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03903)
*Zhipeng Xu,Junhao Ji,Zulong Chen,Zhenghao Liu,Qing Liu,Chunyi Peng,Zubao Qin,Ze Xu,Jianqiang Wan,Jun Tang,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CC-OCR V2基准，包含5大OCR任务、7093个高难度样本，评估14个先进多模态大模型，发现当前模型在真实文档处理场景下性能显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR基准的任务范围与实际应用不匹配，且假设同质的采集条件，缺乏对真实世界文档处理场景的评估。

Method: 构建CC-OCR V2基准，涵盖文本识别、文档解析、文档定位、关键信息提取和文档问答5个OCR核心赛道，包含7093个高难度样本，针对企业级文档处理任务设计，覆盖关键但过往被忽视的困难与边界案例。

Result: 在14个先进多模态大模型上的实验表明，当前模型无法满足真实应用需求，即使最先进的模型在不同任务和场景下性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 当前基准测试性能与真实应用效果之间存在显著差距，CC-OCR V2可有效揭示这一差距，数据集和评估工具已开源。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently shown strong performance on Optical Character Recognition (OCR) tasks, demonstrating their promising capability in document literacy. However, their effectiveness in real-world applications remains underexplored, as existing benchmarks adopt task scopes misaligned with practical applications and assume homogeneous acquisition conditions. To address this gap, we introduce CC-OCR V2, a comprehensive and challenging OCR benchmark tailored to real-world document processing. CC-OCR V2 focuses on practical enterprise document processing tasks and incorporates hard and corner cases that are critical yet underrepresented in prior benchmarks, covering 5 major OCR-centric tracks: text recognition, document parsing, document grounding, key information extraction, and document question answering, comprising 7,093 high-difficulty samples. Extensive experiments on 14 advanced LMMs reveal that current models fall short of real-world application requirements. Even state-of-the-art LMMs exhibit substantial performance degradation across diverse tasks and scenarios. These findings reveal a significant gap between performance on current benchmarks and effectiveness in real-world applications. We release the full dataset and evaluation toolkit at https://github.com/eioss/CC-OCR-V2.

</details>


### [98] [Steer Like the LLM: Activation Steering that Mimics Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03907)
*Geert Heyman,Frederik Vandeputte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR)模型，通过将提示工程转化为激活干预，并学习逐令牌的干预系数，弥合了激活控制方法与提示工程之间的性能差距。在多个基准测试和语言模型上，PSR优于现有激活控制方法，在高连贯性生成控制下甚至与提示工程相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活控制方法在推理时引导语言模型的效果往往不如基于提示的方法，作者旨在探究这一差距的成因并提出弥合方案。

Method: 提出PSR框架：(1)将提示控制形式化为一种激活控制；(2)发现现有激活控制方法不忠实于提示控制机制——提示控制仅在部分令牌上施加强干预；(3)PSR模型从激活值中估计逐令牌的控制系数，并通过模仿提示控制行为进行训练。

Result: 在三个控制基准和多个语言模型上，PSR模型优于现有激活控制方法；在高连贯性控制条件下，PSR在AxBench和人物角色控制任务上与提示工程表现相当甚至更优。

Conclusion: 通过忠实模拟提示控制的逐令牌干预机制，激活控制方法可以达到与提示工程相媲美的效果，弥合了两者之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Large language models can be steered at inference time through prompting or activation interventions, but activation steering methods often underperform compared to prompt-based approaches. We propose a framework that formulates prompt steering as a form of activation steering and investigates whether distilling successful prompt steering behavior into simpler, interpretable models can close this gap. Our analysis reveals that popular activation steering methods are not faithful to the mechanics of prompt steering, which applies strong interventions on some tokens while barely affecting others. Based on these insights, we introduce Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR) models that estimate token-specific steering coefficients from the activations themselves and are trained to imitate prompt-based interventions. Experiments on three steering benchmarks across multiple language models show that PSR models outperform existing activation steering methods, especially when controlling for high-coherence completions, and also compare favorably to prompting on AxBench and persona steering.

</details>


### [99] [Atomic Fact-Checking Increases Clinician Trust in Large Language Model Recommendations for Oncology Decision Support: A Randomized Controlled Trial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03916)
*Lisa C. Adams,Linus Marx,Erik Thiele Orberg,Keno Bressem,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Denise Bernhardt,Markus Graf,Marcus R. Makowski,Stephanie E. Combs,Florian Matthes,Jan C. Peeken*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过随机试验（356名临床医生，7,476个信任评分）发现，原子事实核查（将AI治疗建议分解为可独立验证的声明并链接到源指南文档）相比传统可解释性方法，能显著提高临床医生对AI的信任度（Cohen's d=0.94，信任比例从26.9%提升至66.5%），而传统透明度机制仅呈现剂量-反应梯度改善（d=0.25至0.50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在高风险临床决策中，原子事实核查方法（将AI建议分解为可验证声明并链接源指南）能否比传统可解释性方法更有效地提升临床医生对AI的信任。

Method: 采用随机试验设计，纳入356名临床医生进行7,476次信任评分，比较原子事实核查与传统可解释性方法对临床医生信任的影响，使用Cohen's d量化效应大小。

Result: 原子事实核查产生大效应（Cohen's d=0.94），将信任临床医生的比例从26.9%提升至66.5%；传统透明度机制呈现剂量-反应梯度改善（d=0.25至0.50）。

Conclusion: 在高风险临床决策中，将AI建议分解为可验证声明并链接源指南的原子事实核查方法，比传统可解释性方法能产生显著更高的临床医生信任度。

Abstract: Question: Does atomic fact-checking, which decomposes AI treatment recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guideline documents, increase clinician trust compared to traditional explainability approaches?
  Findings: In this randomized trial of 356 clinicians generating 7,476 trust ratings, atomic fact-checking produced a large effect on trust (Cohen's d = 0.94), increasing the proportion of clinicians expressing trust from 26.9% to 66.5%. Traditional transparency mechanisms showed a dose-response gradient of improvement over baseline (d = 0.25 to 0.50).
  Meaning: Decomposing AI recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guidelines produces substantially higher clinician trust than traditional explainability approaches in high-stakes clinical decisions.

</details>


### [100] [The Counterexample Game: Iterated Conceptual Analysis and Repair in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03936)
*Daniel Drucker,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型能否通过迭代分析和修复链（生成反例→修复定义→重复）进行哲学概念分析。实验发现：LM生成的反例中，LM法官接受率约是人类的2倍，但两者判断中度一致；迭代会生成更冗长的定义而不提升准确性；某些概念难以形成稳定定义。表明LM虽能参与哲学推理，但反例-修复循环很快达到收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念分析（通过反例提炼定义）是哲学方法论的核心，研究者希望探索语言模型能否自主执行这种迭代的哲学推理任务。

Method: 采用迭代分析和修复链：一个模型实例生成反例反驳当前定义，另一个模型实例修复定义，如此循环。对20个概念进行数千轮反例-修复迭代，并引入人类专家和LM法官对反例有效性进行评判。

Result: (1) LM法官接受的反例数量约为人类专家的两倍；(2) 人类与LM之间以及人类之间的有效性判断呈现中等程度一致性；(3) 迭代次数增加导致定义越来越冗长，但并未提高定义准确性；(4) 某些概念难以形成稳定定义。

Conclusion: 语言模型能够参与哲学推理，但反例-修复循环很快达到收益递减，该任务可作为评估LM是否具备持续高级迭代推理能力的测试案例。

Abstract: Conceptual analysis -- proposing definitions and refining them through counterexamples -- is central to philosophical methodology. We study whether language models can perform this task through iterated analysis and repair chains: one model instance generates counterexamples to a proposed definition, another repairs the definition, and the process repeats. Across 20 concepts and thousands of counterexample-repair cycles, we find that, although many LM-generated counterexamples are judged invalid by both expert humans and an LM judge, the LM judge accepts roughly twice as many as humans do. Nonetheless, per-item validity judgments are moderately consistent across humans and between humans and the LM. We further find that extended iteration produces increasingly verbose definitions without improving accuracy. We also see that some concepts resist stable definitions in general. These findings suggest that while LMs can engage in philosophical reasoning, the counterexample-repair loop hits diminishing returns quickly and could be a fruitful test case for evaluating whether LMs can sustain high-level iterated philosophical reasoning.

</details>


### [101] [Feature-Augmented Transformers for Robust AI-Text Detection Across Domains and Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03969)
*Mohamed Mady,Johannes Reschke,Björn Schuller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的AI生成文本检测器，通过注意力机制融合语言特征来提升对分布偏移的鲁棒性。在HC3 PLUS上训练并固定决策阈值，在域内（HC3 PLUS）、跨数据集（M4）和外部检测集上评估，最佳模型DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn在M4上达到85.9%平衡准确率，优于零样本基线最多+7.22个百分点。可读性和词汇特征对鲁棒性贡献最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成文本在跨领域和异构生成管道上大规模产生，导致分布偏移严重，而现有监督二分类检测器对此鲁棒性不足。作者希望通过固定阈值协议和特征增强来评估并提升检测器在实际分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Method: 在HC3 PLUS上训练基于Transformer的检测器，通过最大化验证集平衡准确率校准单一决策阈值并固定。引入注意力机制的语言特征融合（FeatAttn）进行特征增强。在域内（HC3 PLUS）、跨数据集（M4）和外部AI-Text-Detection-Pile上评估，进行多种子实验确认稳定性。

Result: 域内性能接近天花板（最高99.5%平衡准确率），但分布偏移下性能脆弱。最佳模型DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn在M4上达85.9%平衡准确率，多种子实验稳定性高。固定阈值协议下优于零样本基线最多+7.22个百分点。可读性和词汇特征贡献最大。

Conclusion: 特征增强和现代DeBERTa骨干显著优于早期BERT/RoBERTa模型；固定阈值协议提供了更现实和可靠的检测器鲁棒性评估方式。

Abstract: AI-generated text is nowadays produced at scale across domains and heterogeneous generation pipelines, making robustness to distribution shift a central requirement for supervised binary detectors. We train transformer-based detectors on HC3 PLUS and calibrate a single decision threshold by maximising balanced accuracy on held-out validation; this threshold is then kept fixed for all downstream test distributions, revealing domain- and generator-dependent error asymmetries under shift. We evaluate in-domain on HC3 PLUS, under cross-dataset transfer to the multi-domain, multi-generator M4 benchmark, and on the external AI-Text-Detection-Pile. Although base models achieve near-ceiling in-domain performance (up to 99.5% balanced accuracy), performance under shift is brittle and strongly model-dependent. Feature augmentation via attention-based linguistic feature fusion improves transfer, with our best model (DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn) achieving 85.9% balanced accuracy on M4. Multi-seed experiments confirm high stability. Under the same fixed-threshold protocol, our model outperforms strong zero-shot baselines by up to +7.22 points. Category-level ablations further show that readability and vocabulary features contribute most to robustness under shift. Overall, these results demonstrate that feature augmentation and a modern DeBERTa backbone significantly outperform earlier BERT/RoBERTa models, while the fixed-threshold protocol provides a more realistic and informative assessment of practical detector robustness.

</details>


### [102] [Logical Consistency as a Bridge: Improving LLM Hallucination Detection via Label Constraint Modeling between Responses and Self-Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03971)
*Hao Mi,Qiang Sheng,Shaofei Wang,Beizhe Hu,Yifan Sun,Zhengjia Wang,Hengqi Zeng,Yang Li,Danding Wang,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LaaB框架，通过元判断机制将符号标签映射回特征空间，利用自判断语义构建逻辑桥接，实现神经特征与符号推理的协同学习，提升幻觉检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法要么提取微观内在特征进行不确定性量化，要么通过提示词引发宏观自我判断，仅关注单一层面（神经不确定性或符号推理），未能利用两者间的内在耦合关系与相互依赖性。

Method: 提出LaaB（Logical Consistency-as-a-Bridge）框架：设计元判断过程将符号标签映射回特征空间；利用响应与元判断标签之间的逻辑一致性（相同或相反）作为桥接；通过互学习机制对齐并整合双视角信号。

Result: 在4个公开数据集、4种LLM、8个基线上进行广泛实验，LaaB展现出优越性能。

Conclusion: LaaB通过逻辑一致性桥接神经特征与符号判断，有效利用双视角的相互依赖关系，实现了更全面的幻觉检测。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are prone to factual hallucinations, risking their reliability in real-world applications. Existing hallucination detectors mainly extract micro-level intrinsic patterns for uncertainty quantification or elicit macro-level self-judgments through verbalized prompts. However, these methods address only a single facet of the hallucination, focusing either on implicit neural uncertainty or explicit symbolic reasoning, thereby treating these inherently coupled behaviors in isolation and failing to exploit their interdependence for a holistic view. In this paper, we propose LaaB (Logical Consistency-as-a-Bridge), a framework that bridges neural features and symbolic judgments for hallucination detection. LaaB introduces a "meta-judgment" process to map symbolic labels back into the feature space. By leveraging the inherent logical bridge where response and meta-judgment labels are either the same or opposite based on the self-judgment's semantics, LaaB aligns and integrates dual-view signals via mutual learning and enhances the hallucination detection. Extensive experiments on 4 public datasets, across 4 LLMs, against 8 baselines demonstrate the superiority of LaaB.

</details>


### [103] [EQUITRIAGE: A Fairness Audit of Gender Bias in LLM-Based Emergency Department Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03998)
*Richard J. Young,Alice M. Matthews*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对五种LLM在急诊科分诊中的性别公平性进行审计，发现在374,275次评估中，所有模型均产生超过5%阈值的性别翻转率（9.9%-43.8%），其中DeepSeek和Gemini表现出方向性女性分诊不足。研究表明群体均等、反事实不变性和性别校准是三种不同的公平性属性，干预效果具有模型依赖性，部署前应进行逐模型反事实审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科分诊中存在持续的性别差异，而医院正试点使用LLM作为分诊决策支持工具，因此需要评估这些模型是复制还是减轻已知的性别偏见。

Method: 构建EQUITRIAGE公平性审计框架，对五种LLM在18,714个MIMIC-IV-ED病例上进行了374,275次评估，使用四种提示策略。创建了9,346对原始病例与性别反转的反事实病例，测量翻转率、校准偏差、方向性分诊不足等指标，并进行性别盲化和年龄保留消融实验。

Result: 所有模型的翻转率均超过预注册的5%阈值（9.9%-43.8%）。DeepSeek和Gemini表现出方向性女性分诊不足（F/M比分别为2.15:1和1.34:1）。性别盲化将Gemini的翻转率降至0.5%，但DeepSeek仍有残留偏差（F/M 1.25）。思维链提示降低了所有模型的准确性。

Conclusion: 群体均等、反事实不变性和性别校准是不同的公平性属性；干预有效性具有模型依赖性；反事实审计应作为临床部署前的标准步骤。

Abstract: Emergency department triage assigns patients an acuity score that determines treatment priority, and clinical evidence documents persistent gender disparities in human acuity assessment. As hospitals pilot large language models (LLMs) as triage decision support, a critical question is whether these models reproduce or mitigate known biases. We present EQUITRIAGE, a fairness audit of LLM-based ESI assignment evaluating five models (Gemini-3-Flash, Nemotron-3-Super, DeepSeek-V3.1, Mistral-Small-3.2, GPT-4.1-Nano) across 374,275 evaluations on 18,714 MIMIC-IV-ED vignettes under four prompt strategies. Of 9,368 originals, 9,346 are paired with a gender-swapped counterfactual. All five models produced flip rates above a pre-registered 5% threshold (9.9% to 43.8%). Two showed directional female undertriage (DeepSeek F/M 2.15:1, Gemini 1.34:1); two were near-parity; one had high sensitivity with weak male-direction asymmetry. DeepSeek's directional bias coexisted with a low outcome-linked calibration gap (0.013 against MIMIC-IV admission), a Chouldechova-style dissociation between within-group calibration and between-pair counterfactual invariance. Demographic blinding reduced Gemini's flip rate to 0.5%; an age-preserving blind variant left DeepSeek with residual F/M 1.25, implicating age as a residual channel. Chain-of-thought prompting degraded accuracy for all five models. A two-model ablation reveals opposite underlying mechanisms for the same directional phenotype: in Gemini the signal is emergent in the combined name+gender swap, while in DeepSeek the gender token alone carries it. EQUITRIAGE shows that group parity, counterfactual invariance, and gender calibration are distinct fairness properties, that intervention effectiveness is model-dependent, and that per-model counterfactual auditing should precede clinical deployment.

</details>


### [104] [Rethinking Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval: Evaluating and Advancing Retrievers in Agentic Search Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04018)
*Yilun Zhao,Jinbiao Wei,Tingyu Song,Siyue Zhang,Chen Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BRIGHT-Pro基准和RTriever-4B检索器，用于解决推理密集型检索中多证据组合评估与训练不足的问题，实验表明方面感知和智能体评估能揭示标准指标隐藏的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理密集型检索工作在评估和训练上均有限：基准如BRIGHT仅提供狭窄的金标集并孤立评估检索器，合成训练语料常优化单段相关性而非证据组合构建，缺乏对多证据组合能力的评估与训练。

Method: 构建BRIGHT-Pro专家标注基准（多维度金标证据+静态与智能体两种评估协议），构建RTriever-Synth方面分解合成语料（互补正例+正例条件化难负例），基于Qwen3-Embedding-4B进行LoRA微调得到RTriever-4B。

Result: 实验涵盖词法、通用和推理密集型检索器，表明方面感知和智能体评估能揭示标准指标隐藏的行为，RTriever-4B显著优于其基础模型。

Conclusion: 现有的标准检索评估指标无法充分反映推理密集型检索的真实表现，而本文提出的方面感知评估、智能体搜索协议以及相应的训练方法能更有效地评估和提升检索器在推理场景下的多证据组合能力。

Abstract: Reasoning-intensive retrieval aims to surface evidence that supports downstream reasoning rather than merely matching topical similarity. This capability is increasingly important for agentic search systems, where retrievers must provide complementary evidence across iterative search and synthesis. However, existing work remains limited on both evaluation and training: benchmarks such as BRIGHT provide narrow gold sets and evaluate retrievers in isolation, while synthetic training corpora often optimize single-passage relevance rather than evidence portfolio construction. We introduce BRIGHT-Pro, an expert-annotated benchmark that expands each query with multi-aspect gold evidence and evaluates retrievers under both static and agentic search protocols. We further construct RTriever-Synth, an aspect-decomposed synthetic corpus that generates complementary positives and positive-conditioned hard negatives, and use it to LoRA fine-tune RTriever-4B from Qwen3-Embedding-4B. Experiments across lexical, general-purpose, and reasoning-intensive retrievers show that aspect-aware and agentic evaluation expose behaviors hidden by standard metrics, while RTriever-4B substantially improves over its base model.

</details>


### [105] [Safety and accuracy follow different scaling laws in clinical large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04039)
*Sebastian Wind,Tri-Thien Nguyen,Jeta Sopa,Mahshad Lotfinia,Sebastian Bickelhaup,Michael Uder,Harald Köstler,Gerhard Wellein,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn,Andreas Maier,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SaFE-Scale框架，系统评估临床大语言模型在多种部署条件下的安全性，发现证据质量（而非模型规模或推理计算量）是安全性的关键决定因素，标准RAG和智能体RAG未能复现干净证据带来的安全提升，临床LLM安全是部署属性的结果而非规模扩展的被动产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床LLM研究通常通过扩展模型规模、上下文长度、检索复杂度或推理计算量来提升性能，隐含假设是更高的准确率意味着更安全的行为。但这一假设在医学领域不成立——少数高置信度、高风险或与证据矛盾的错误可能比平均基准性能更重要。因此，需要系统评估临床LLM安全性随不同因素变化的规律。

Method: 提出SaFE-Scale框架，用于测量临床LLM安全性随模型规模、证据质量、检索策略、上下文暴露和推理计算量的变化。为实例化该框架，构建RadSaFE-200基准（200道放射学多选题，含临床医生定义的干净证据、矛盾证据及选项级高风险错误、不安全答案和证据矛盾标签）。在34个本地部署的LLM上评估六种部署条件：闭卷零样本、干净证据、矛盾证据、标准RAG、智能体RAG和最大上下文提示。

Result: 干净证据带来最强改进：平均准确率从73.5%提升至94.1%，高风险错误从12.0%降至2.6%，矛盾从12.7%降至2.3%，危险过度自信从8.0%降至1.6%。标准RAG和智能体RAG未复现该安全特征：智能体RAG虽提升准确率和降低矛盾，但高风险错误和危险过度自信仍然较高。最大上下文提示增加延迟但未缩小安全差距，额外推理计算仅带来有限收益。最坏情况分析显示临床后果性错误集中在少数问题上。

Conclusion: 临床LLM安全性并非规模扩展的被动结果，而是由证据质量、检索设计、上下文构建和集体失败行为共同塑造的部署属性。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs are often scaled by increasing model size, context length, retrieval complexity, or inference-time compute, with the implicit expectation that higher accuracy implies safer behavior. This assumption is incomplete in medicine, where a few confident, high-risk, or evidence-contradicting errors can matter more than average benchmark performance. We introduce SaFE-Scale, a framework for measuring how clinical LLM safety changes across model scale, evidence quality, retrieval strategy, context exposure, and inference-time compute. To instantiate this framework, we introduce RadSaFE-200, a Radiology Safety-Focused Evaluation benchmark of 200 multiple-choice questions with clinician-defined clean evidence, conflict evidence, and option-level labels for high-risk error, unsafe answer, and evidence contradiction. We evaluated 34 locally deployed LLMs across six deployment conditions: closed-book prompting (zero-shot), clean evidence, conflict evidence, standard RAG, agentic RAG, and max-context prompting. Clean evidence produced the strongest improvement, increasing mean accuracy from 73.5% to 94.1%, while reducing high-risk error from 12.0% to 2.6%, contradiction from 12.7% to 2.3%, and dangerous overconfidence from 8.0% to 1.6%. Standard RAG and agentic RAG did not reproduce this safety profile: agentic RAG improved accuracy over standard RAG and reduced contradiction, but high-risk error and dangerous overconfidence remained elevated. Max-context prompting increased latency without closing the safety gap, and additional inference-time compute produced only limited gains. Worst-case analysis showed that clinically consequential errors concentrated in a small subset of questions. Clinical LLM safety is therefore not a passive consequence of scaling, but a deployment property shaped by evidence quality, retrieval design, context construction, and collective failure behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [CreativityBench: Evaluating Agent Creative Reasoning via Affordance-Based Tool Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02910)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Bingxiang He,Jeonghwan Kim,Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Aditi Tiwari,Dwip Dalal,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Yunzhu Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CreativityBench基准，用于评估大语言模型在创造性工具使用方面的能力，基于含4K实体和150K+属性标注的大规模知识库生成14K任务。对10个前沿LLM的评估显示，模型能选择合理物体，但无法识别正确部件、属性及物理机制，且模型扩展、推理增强策略均改善有限，表明创造性工具使用仍是当前模型的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理和交互任务上表现出色，但其创造性解决问题的能力尚未被充分探索。本文旨在通过创造性工具使用这一维度来系统评估LLM的创造性思维能力。

Method: 构建大规模属性知识库（4K实体，150K+属性标注），连接物体、部件、属性和可操作用途；基于该知识库生成14K接地任务，要求模型在约束条件下识别非显而易见但物理上可行的解决方案。

Result: 10个SOTA LLM（包括闭源和开源）的评估表明：模型能选择合理的物体，但无法识别正确的部件、属性和底层物理机制；模型规模扩展的收益迅速饱和；强通用推理能力不能可靠迁移到创造性属性发现；常见的推理时策略（如思维链）带来的提升有限。

Conclusion: 创造性工具使用仍是当前大语言模型的重大挑战，CreativityBench为研究这一缺失的智能维度提供了有用的测试平台，对未来的智能体规划和推理模块具有潜在影响。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have led to strong performance on reasoning and environment-interaction tasks, yet their ability for creative problem-solving remains underexplored. We study this capability through the lens of creative tool use, where a model repurposes available objects by reasoning about their affordances and attributes rather than relying on canonical usage. As a first step, we introduce CreativityBench, a benchmark for evaluating affordance-based creativity in LLMs. To this end, we build a large-scale affordance knowledge base (KB) with 4K entities and 150K+ affordance annotations, explicitly linking objects, parts, attributes, and actionable uses. Building on this KB, we generate 14K grounded tasks that require identifying non-obvious yet physically plausible solutions under constraints. Evaluations across 10 state-of-the-art LLMs, including closed and open-source models, show that models can often select a plausible object, but fail to identify the correct parts, their affordances, and the underlying physical mechanism needed to solve the task, leading to a significant drop in performance. Furthermore, improvements from model scaling quickly saturate, strong general reasoning does not reliably translate to creative affordance discovery, and common inference-time strategies such as Chain-of-Thought yield limited gains. These results suggest that creative tool use remains a major challenge for current models, and that CreativityBench provides a useful testbed for studying this missing dimension of intelligence, with potential implications for planning and reasoning modules in future agents.

</details>


### [107] [Stable Agentic Control: Tool-Mediated LLM Architecture for Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03034)
*Kerri Prinos,Lilianne Brush,Cameron Denton,Zhanqi Wang,Joshua Knox,Snehal Antani,Anton Foltz,Amy Villaseñor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种工具中介的架构，使LLM智能体在对抗压力下配置EDR策略时具备形式化安全保证。该架构通过确定性工具（Stackelberg最优响应、贝叶斯观测器更新、攻击图原语）和有限动作目录强制执行，并使用Lean 4机器验证的复合Lyapunov函数提供可控性、可观性和ISS鲁棒性证明。在282个真实企业攻击图上验证成立，Claude Sonnet 4控制器相比贪婪基线降低攻击者期望收益59%且零方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法为高对抗压力下的高风险决策提供形式化保证，安全运营中心（SOC）需要可证明安全的EDR策略配置方法。

Method: 1) 工具中介架构：LLM智能体通过确定性工具（Stackelberg最优响应、贝叶斯更新、攻击图原语）行动，输出受有限动作目录约束；2) 在Lean 4中机器验证复合Lyapunov函数，证明可控性、观测性和输入-状态稳定性（ISS）；3) 在真实企业攻击图和攻防遥测数据上实验验证。

Result: 1) 282个真实企业攻击图上所有形式化声明成立且有裕度；2) Claude Sonnet 4控制器使攻击者期望收益降低59%（相对贪婪基线），40次运行零方差；3) Claude Haiku 4.5控制器虽收敛到次优但保持目录有界；4) LLM非确定性提供策略创造性探索，同时工具架构保证系统稳定性。

Conclusion: 工具中介架构是确保LLM智能体在高对抗高风险环境中具备形式化安全保证的有效方案，系统稳定性不依赖于控制器能力，非确定性有助于策略探索而工具架构维持稳定性。

Abstract: Agentic systems involved in high-stake decision-making under adversarial pressure need formal guarantees not offered by existing approaches. Motivated by the operational needs of security operations centers (SOCs) that must configure endpoint detection and response (EDR) policies under adversarial pressure, we present a tool-mediated architecture: LLM agents use deterministic tools (Stackelberg best-response, Bayesian observer updates, attack-graph primitives) and select from finite action catalogs enforced at the tool-output interface. A composite Lyapunov function machine-checked in Lean 4 with zero sorry certifies controllability, observability from asymmetric sensor data, and Input-to-State Stability (ISS) robustness under intelligent adversarial disturbance, with two corollaries extending the certificate to any controller or adversary from the catalogs. On 282 real enterprise attack graphs, the claims hold with margin. On paired offensive/defensive telemetry, a tool-mediated Claude Sonnet 4 controller reduces the attacker's expected payoff (game value) by 59% relative to a deterministic greedy baseline, with zero variance across 40 runs at four temperatures. A Claude Haiku 4.5 controller converges to suboptimal game values but stays catalog-bounded over an additional 40 runs, demonstrating that architectural stability is not dependent on the controller capability. The LLM agent's non-determinism furthers creative exploration of strategies, while the tool-mediated architecture ensures system stability.

</details>


### [108] [Computing Thiele Rules on Interval Elections and their Generalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03067)
*Dimitris Avramidis,Alexandra Lassota,Ulrike Schmidt-Kraepelin,Adrian Vetta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于批准的委员会投票中Thiele规则（特别是比例批准投票PAV）的计算复杂性。尽管在候选区间（CI）域上可以通过具有全幺模约束矩阵的线性规划（LP）多项式时间求解，但在选民区间（VI）域上该LP仍存在最优整数解，作者提供了快速算法。该技术扩展到选民-候选区间（VCI）域和线性一致（LC）域，并证明了LC严格包含VCI。最后，树形推广域上Thiele规则是NP-hard的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thiele规则在一般情况下的计算是NP-hard的，但在结构化偏好下可能更易处理。CI域上已有多项式时间算法，但VI域上的复杂性是悬而未决的开放问题。本文旨在解决VI域及更一般域上的计算复杂性，并厘清VCI与LC域之间的关系。

Method: 通过线性规划（LP）分析约束矩阵结构，证明VI域上虽矩阵非全幺模，但LP仍存在最优整数解，并设计相应快速算法。利用图论工具建立VCI与LC域的关系，证明LC严格包含VCI。构造树形推广域并证明其上Thiele规则计算为NP-hard。

Result: 解决了VI域上Thiele规则计算复杂性的开放问题，证明了标准LP存在最优整数解并给出快速算法。证明了LC域严格包含VCI域，并提供了LC域的新等价定义。树形推广域上Thiele规则计算是NP-hard的。

Conclusion: Thiele规则在多种结构化偏好域（CI、VI、VCI、LC）上可多项式时间求解，但树形推广会重新导致NP-hard。VCI与LC域的关系被澄清，LC是更一般的结构。这为基于批准的委员会投票在实际应用中利用偏好结构提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Approval-based committee voting has received significant attention in the social choice community. Among the studied rules, Thiele rules, and especially Proportional Approval Voting (PAV), stand out for desirable properties such as proportional representation, Pareto optimality, and support monotonicity. Their main drawback is that computing a Thiele outcome is NP-hard in general.
  A glimpse of hope comes from the fact that Thiele rules are better behaved under structured preferences. On the candidate interval (CI) domain, they are computable in polynomial time via a linear program (LP) that has a totally unimodular constraint matrix. Surprisingly, this approach fails for the related voter interval (VI) domain, and the complexity of the problem has repeatedly been posed as an open question. Our main result resolves this question: although the relevant matrix is not totally unimodular, the ``standard'' LP still admits at least one optimal integral solution, and we provide a fast algorithm for finding it.
  Our technique naturally extends to the voter-candidate interval (VCI) domain, also known as the 1-dimensional voter-candidate range (1D-VCR) domain, and to the linearly consistent (LC) domain, both of which generalize the candidate and voter interval domains. Although both the VCI and LC domains have been studied in social choice, their relationship was unknown. We show, through connections to graph theory, that LC strictly contains VCI. We also provide an alternative definition of LC that is closer in spirit to VCI and has a natural interpretation in approval elections; this equivalence may be of independent interest. Finally, we study an alternative tree-based generalization of VCI and show that Thiele rules become NP-hard to compute on this domain.

</details>


### [109] [Making the Invisible Visible: Understanding the Mismatch Between Organizational Goals and Worker Experiences in AI Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03078)
*Christine P. Lee,Min Kyung Lee,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对医疗、金融和管理领域与AI系统日常互动的专业人员访谈，揭示了组织引入AI时忽视工人体验导致的采纳失败问题，确定了关键障碍包括可用性差、期望错位、控制权有限和沟通不足，并提出在个体、任务和组织层面调整策略以促进AI与真实工作实践的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织引入AI常因工人抵制和难以整合而失败，根源在于被期望与AI协作的工人在AI设计和使用的决策中被忽视，缺乏对他们实际体验的关注。

Method: 对医疗、金融和管理领域每天与AI系统互动的专业人员进行访谈，分析组织期望与工人实际体验之间的脱节。

Result: 识别出关键障碍：可用性和互操作性差、期望错位、控制权有限、沟通不足，揭示了AI实施与工人不断变化的需求、任务和工作流程之间存在鸿沟。

Conclusion: 成功采纳AI需要将工人视为AI整合的中心，并应在个体、任务和组织三个层面提出适应策略，以更好地将AI系统与真实工作实践对齐。

Abstract: While AI is often introduced into organizations to drive innovation and efficiency, many adoption efforts fail as workers resist and struggle to integrate these systems. These failures point to a deeper issue: workers, the very people expected to collaborate with AI, are often invisible in decisions about how AI is designed and used. Drawing on interviews with professionals who interact with AI systems daily in healthcare, finance, and management, we examine the disconnect between organizational expectations and worker experiences. We identify key barriers, including poor usability and interoperability, misaligned expectations, limited control, and insufficient communication. These challenges highlight a gap between how organizations implement AI and the evolving worker needs, tasks, and workflows that it fails to support. We argue that successful adoption requires recognizing workers as central to AI integration and propose adaptation strategies at the individual, task, and organizational levels to better align AI systems with real-world practices.

</details>


### [110] [Programmatic Context Augmentation for LLM-based Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03101)
*Hao Liu,Xiao-Wen Yang,Atharva Sehgal,Yixin Wang,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大型语言模型的新型进化搜索框架，通过编程化上下文增强（允许与数据集进行基于代码的交互）来提取更丰富的信息，从而提升符号回归的性能和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的符号回归方法仅依赖标量评估指标（如均方误差）作为搜索过程中的唯一反馈来源，忽略了数据集中嵌入的丰富信息，导致搜索效率低下。

Method: 提出LLM进化搜索框架，通过编程化上下文增强（programmatic context augmentation），使模型能够以代码形式与数据集交互，主动进行数据分析并提取超越聚合评估分数的信息信号。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench等高级基准测试上，相比于强基线方法，该方法在效率和准确率上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过编程化上下文增强赋予LLM与数据集直接交互的能力，能够有效提升符号回归的搜索质量，突破了传统标量反馈的局限。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR), the task of discovering mathematical expressions that best describe a given dataset, remains a fundamental challenge in scientific discovery. Traditional approaches, primarily based on genetic algorithms and related evolutionary methods, have proven useful but suffer from scalability and expressivity limitations. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based evolutionary search methods have been introduced into SR and show promise. However, existing LLM-based approaches typically rely on scalar evaluation metrics, such as mean squared error, as the sole source of feedback during the search process, thereby overlooking the rich information embedded in the dataset. To address this limitation, we propose a novel LLM-based evolutionary search framework that incorporates programmatic context augmentation. By enabling code-based interactions with the dataset, our method can actively perform data analysis and extract informative signals, beyond aggregated evaluation scores. We evaluate our framework on advanced benchmarks, such as LLM-SRBench, and demonstrate superior efficiency and accuracy compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [111] [Learning Correct Behavior from Examples: Validating Sequential Execution in Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03159)
*Reshabh K Sharma,Gaurav Mittal,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种从少量（2-10条）成功执行轨迹自动学习正确行为并验证新执行的新算法，结合编译器理论中的支配者分析和多模态大语言模型，在UI测试、代码生成和机器人流程等多个领域实现了高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体日益复杂，验证其序列行为面临重大挑战。传统方法需要手动规约、精确序列匹配或大量训练样本，缺乏高效且可解释的自动验证手段。

Method: 结合编译器理论中的支配者分析与多模态大语言模型的语义理解，识别关键状态并处理非确定性行为；使用前缀树接收器构建通用真值模型，通过多层次等价检测合并轨迹，并通过拓扑子序列匹配验证新执行。

Result: 在受控实验中，仅使用3条训练轨迹就能高准确率检测产品缺陷和误报成功案例，并提供可解释的验证结果和覆盖度指标。

Conclusion: 该方法在UI测试、代码生成和机器人流程等多领域均有效，为自主智能体的行为验证提供了一种样本高效、可解释且跨领域适用的解决方案。

Abstract: As autonomous agents become increasingly sophisticated, validating their sequential behavior presents a significant challenge. Traditional testing approaches require manual specification, exact sequence matching, or thousands of training examples.
